		name = state_atheism.1.a
		default_option = yes
		# lose enactment chance
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = opium_of_the_masses_enactment
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = state_atheism.1.b
		# radicals from SoL
		add_modifier = {
			name = opium_of_the_masses_mod
			days = long_modifier_time
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = state_atheism.1.c
		trigger = {
			market = {
				mg:opium = {
					market_goods_consumption > 100
				}
			}
			any_primary_culture = {
				NOT = { has_cultural_obsession = opium }
			}
		}
		random_primary_culture = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_cultural_obsession = opium }
			}
			add_cultural_obsession = opium
		}
	}
}

# Advancement event - Society of the Godless
state_atheism.2 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:godless_state

	title = state_atheism.2.t
	desc = state_atheism.2.d
	flavor = state_atheism.2.f

	category = enactment

	event_image = {
		video = "votp_conspiring"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_skull.dds"

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		is_enacting_law = law_type:law_state_atheism
		any_scope_state = {
			is_incorporated = yes
			has_building = building_university
			any_scope_pop = {
				is_pop_type = academics
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = {
			currently_enacting_law = scope:current_law_scope
		}
	}

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	immediate = {
		currently_enacting_law = {
			save_scope_as = current_law_scope
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				is_incorporated = yes
				has_building = building_university
				any_scope_pop = {
					is_pop_type = academics
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = godless_state
			random_scope_building = {
				limit = {
					is_building_type = building_university
				}
				save_scope_as = godless_uni
			}
			random_scope_pop = {
				limit = {
					is_pop_type = academics
				}
				save_scope_as = godless_pop
			}
		}
		ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= {
			save_scope_as = godless_ig
			leader = {
				save_scope_as = godless_leader
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = state_atheism.2.a
		default_option = yes
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = society_of_the_godless_mod
			multiplier = 1
		}
	}
	option = { # gain academic loyalists
		name = state_atheism.2.b
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = society_of_the_godless_mod
			multiplier = 1.5
		}
		scope:godless_state = {
			add_loyalists_in_state = {
				pop_type = academics
				value = very_large_radicals
			}
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				religion = root.religion
				value = medium_radicals
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # new atheist intelligentsia leader
		name = state_atheism.2.c
		trigger = {
			ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= {
				ig_counts_as_marginal = no
				leader = {
					NOT = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_nihilist }
				}
			}
		}
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = society_of_the_godless_mod
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = godless_ig_leader
			create_character = {
				ig_leader = yes
				interest_group = ig_intelligentsia
				religion = rel:atheist
				ideology = ideology_nihilist
			}
		}
	}
}

# Stall event - Works of Charity
state_atheism.3 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT

	title = state_atheism.3.t
	desc = state_atheism.3.d

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		is_enacting_law = law_type:law_state_atheism
		any_interest_group = {
			is_in_government = no
			ig_counts_as_marginal = no
			law_stance = {
				law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type
				value < neutral
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = {
			currently_enacting_law = scope:current_law_scope
		}
	}

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	immediate = {
		currently_enacting_law = {
			save_scope_as = current_law_scope
		}
		random_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				is_in_government = no
				ig_counts_as_marginal = no
				law_stance = {
					law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type
					value < neutral
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = works_of_charity_ig
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = state_atheism.3.a
		default_option = yes
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = unpopular_attacks_on_charities
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = state_atheism.3.b
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_law = law_type:law_no_social_security }
		}

		random_list = {
			1 = {
				add_enactment_modifier = {
					name = state_welfare_disparaged
				}
			}
			1 = {
				add_enactment_modifier = {
					name = state_welfare_promoted
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = state_atheism.3.c
		trigger = { has_law = law_type:law_no_social_security }

		add_modifier = {
			name = ad_hoc_government_charities
			days = short_modifier_time
		}

		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = imitating_charities
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = state_atheism.3.dd

		scope:works_of_charity_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = popular_works_of_charity
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
}

#Extraterritoriality in God's Nation - Stall
state_atheism.4 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT

	title = state_atheism.4.t
	desc = state_atheism.4.d
	flavor = state_atheism.4.f

	category = enactment

	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_politicians_arguing"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_protest.dds"

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		is_enacting_law = law_type:law_state_atheism
		any_interest_group = {
			law_stance = {
				law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type
				value > neutral
			}
		}
		any_country = {
			any_neighbouring_state = {
				owner = root
			}
			NOT = {
				has_law = law_type:law_state_atheism
			}
			is_country_type = recognized
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = {
			currently_enacting_law = scope:current_law_scope
		}
	}

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	immediate = {
		currently_enacting_law = {
			save_scope_as = current_law_scope
		}
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				any_neighbouring_state = {
					owner = root
				}
				NOT = {
					has_law = law_type:law_state_atheism
				}
				is_country_type = recognized
			}
			save_scope_as = missionary_nation
		}
		ig:ig_devout ?= {
			save_scope_as = devout_ig
		}
		random_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				law_stance = {
					law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type
					value > neutral
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = atheist_ig
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = state_atheism.4.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:atheist_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_caved_to_theists
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = modifier_expelled_missionaries_tolerance
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = state_atheism.4.b
		create_incident = {
			country = scope:missionary_nation
			value = 10
		}
		scope:missionary_nation = {
			change_relations = { country = root value = -15 }
		}
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = modifier_expelled_missionaries_infamy
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = state_atheism.4.c
		ig:ig_devout ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_expelled_devouts_friends
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
		add_radicals = {
			value = small_radicals
			pop_type = clergymen
		}
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = modifier_expelled_missionaries_devout
		}
	}
}

state_atheism.5 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT

	title = state_atheism.5.t
	desc = state_atheism.5.d
	flavor = state_atheism.5.f

	category = enactment

	event_image = {
		video = "middleeast_middleclass_cafe"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_scales.dds"

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		is_enacting_law = law_type:law_state_atheism
		ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= {
			ig_counts_as_marginal = no
			leader = {
				OR = {
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_radical
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_nihilist
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_communist
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_vanguardist
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_anarchist
				}
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = {
			currently_enacting_law = scope:current_law_scope
		}
	}

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	immediate = {
		currently_enacting_law = {
			save_scope_as = current_law_scope
		}
		ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= {
			save_scope_as = intelligentsia_ig
		}
		ig:ig_devout ?= {
			save_scope_as = devout_ig
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = state_atheism.5.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:devout_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_retreat_on_faith
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = modifier_great_retreat_on_faith
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = state_atheism.5.b
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = modifier_atheist_progressivism
		}
		add_radicals = {
			value = small_radicals
			interest_group = scope:devout_ig
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = state_atheism.5.c
		trigger = {
			enactment_phase < 2
		}
		highlighted_option = yes
		add_enactment_phase = 1
		add_radicals = {
			value = medium_radicals
			interest_group = scope:devout_ig
		}
	}
}

				strata = lower
			}
		}

		}
	}

﻿namespace = tax

# Taxation is theft
# Stall event for any tax law
tax.1 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT

	title = tax.1.t
	desc = tax.1.d
	flavor = tax.1.f

	category = enactment

	event_image = {
		video = "middleeast_courtroom_upheaval"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_protest.dds"

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			is_enacting_law = law_type:law_proportional_taxation
			is_enacting_law = law_type:law_graduated_taxation
			is_enacting_law = law_type:law_land_based_taxation
			is_enacting_law = law_type:law_per_capita_based_taxation
		}
		any_political_movement = {
			law_stance = {
				law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type
				value < neutral
			}
		}
		any_interest_group = {
			law_stance = {
				law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type
				value < neutral
			}
		}
		NOT = { has_modifier = taxation_is_theft }
		NOT = {
			currently_enacting_law = {
				has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_short_term_tax_cuts
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_political_movement = {
			limit = {
				law_stance = {
					law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type
					value < neutral
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = antitax_rebels
		}
		random_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				law_stance = {
					law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type
					value < neutral
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = antitax_ig
		}
		currently_enacting_law = {
			save_scope_as = current_law_scope
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = { currently_enacting_law = scope:current_law_scope }
	}

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	option = {
		name = tax.1.a
		default_option = yes
		# lose success chance
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = taxation_is_theft
			multiplier = 3
		}
		# movement gets angrier
		scope:antitax_rebels = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = antitax_anger
				days = long_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # promise to keep taxes low
		name = tax.1.b
		# lose less success chance
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = taxation_is_theft
		}
		currently_enacting_law = {
			add_amendment = {
				type = amendment_short_term_tax_cuts
				sponsor = scope:antitax_ig
				cooldown = 30
			}
		}
	}
}

# Advance event - Supporting the War Effort
tax.2 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT

	title = tax.2.t
	desc = tax.2.d
	flavor = tax.2.f

	category = enactment

	event_image = {
		video = "europenorthamerica_before_the_battle"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_military.dds"

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			is_enacting_law = law_type:law_proportional_taxation
			is_enacting_law = law_type:law_graduated_taxation
			is_enacting_law = law_type:law_per_capita_based_taxation
		}
		any_political_movement = {
			law_stance = {
				law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type
				value < neutral
			}
		}
		is_at_war = yes
		any_diplomatic_play = {
			is_war = yes
			any_scope_play_involved = {
				THIS = ROOT
			}
		}
		NOT = { has_modifier = patriotic_taxation }
	}

	immediate = {
		currently_enacting_law = {
    		save_scope_as = current_law_scope
    	}
		random_political_movement = {
			limit = {
				law_stance = {
					law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type
					value < neutral
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = antitax_rebels
		}
		ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
			save_scope_as = patriotic_military
		}
		random_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				is_war = yes
				any_scope_play_involved = {
					THIS = ROOT
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = war_scope
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = { currently_enacting_law = scope:current_law_scope }
	}

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	option = { # cast dissenters as unpatriotic
		name = tax.2.a
		default_option = yes
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = patriotic_taxation
		}
		# reduce movement support substantially
		scope:antitax_rebels = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = unpatriotic_movement
				days = long_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # cast the law as patriotic
		name = tax.2.b
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = patriotic_taxation
		}
		scope:patriotic_military ={
			add_modifier = {
				name = patriocitc_taxation_ig
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		add_loyalists = {
			pop_type = soldiers
			value = 0.1
		}
		add_loyalists = {
			pop_type = officers
			value = 0.1
		}
	}
}

# Debate event - Death and Taxes
tax.3 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT

	title = tax.3.t
	desc = tax.3.d
	flavor = tax.3.f

	category = enactment

	event_image = {
		video = "africa_leader_arguing"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_skull.dds"

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		any_interest_group = {
			is_in_government = yes
			law_stance = {
				law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type
				value > neutral
			}
			leader = {
				is_busy = no
				is_immortal = no
			}
		}
		OR = {
			is_enacting_law = law_type:law_consumption_based_taxation
			is_enacting_law = law_type:law_proportional_taxation
			is_enacting_law = law_type:law_graduated_taxation
			is_enacting_law = law_type:law_land_based_taxation
			is_enacting_law = law_type:law_per_capita_based_taxation
		}
		NOT = {
			has_modifier = death_and_taxes_mod
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		currently_enacting_law = {
    		save_scope_as = current_law_scope
    	}
    	random_interest_group = {
    		limit = {
    			is_in_government = yes
				law_stance = {
					law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type
					value > neutral
				}
				leader = {
					is_busy = no
					is_immortal = no
				}
    		}
    		leader = {
    			save_scope_as = death_and_taxes_leader
    		}
    	}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = { currently_enacting_law = scope:current_law_scope }
	}

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	option = { # a mourning period would be appropriate
		name = tax.3.a
		default_option = yes
		# slower but more likely
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = death_and_taxes_mod
		}
		scope:death_and_taxes_leader = {
			kill_character = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = tax.3.b # push on
		# faster but less likely
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = death_and_taxes_mod
			multiplier = -1
		}
		scope:death_and_taxes_leader = {
			kill_character = yes
		}
	}
}

# Stall - Popular social bandit
tax.4 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT

	title = tax.4.t
	desc = tax.4.desc
	flavor = tax.4.f

	category = enactment

	event_image = {
		video = "rp1_social_banditry"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			is_enacting_law = law_type:law_land_based_taxation
			is_enacting_law = law_type:law_per_capita_based_taxation
		}
		any_scope_state = {
			is_incorporated = yes
			any_scope_pop = {
				standard_of_living < 15
				strata = lower
				pop_acceptance >= acceptance_status_4
			}
		}
		NOT = { has_modifier = social_banditry }
	}

	immediate = {
		currently_enacting_law = {
    		save_scope_as = current_law_scope
    	}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				is_incorporated = yes
				any_scope_pop = {
					standard_of_living < 15
					strata = lower
					pop_acceptance >= acceptance_status_4
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = bandit_state
			random_scope_pop = {
	    		limit = {
	    			standard_of_living < 15
					strata = lower
					pop_acceptance >= acceptance_status_4
	    		}
	    		save_scope_as = bandit_pop
	    	}
	    }
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = { currently_enacting_law = scope:current_law_scope }
	}

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	option = { # hunt down the bandit
		name = tax.4.a
		default_option = yes
		random_list = {
			1 = {
				add_enactment_modifier = {
					name = social_banditry
					multiplier = -1
				}
			}
			1 = {
				add_enactment_modifier = {
					name = social_banditry
				}
			}
			1 = {
				add_enactment_modifier = {
					name = social_banditry
					multiplier = 2
				}
			}
		}
		scope:bandit_state = {
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = 0.25
				strata = lower
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # hunt him down with trained police
		name = tax.4.b
		highlighted_option = yes
		trigger = {
			institution_investment_level = {
				institution = institution_police
				value >= 3
			}
		}
		random_list = {
			1 = {
				add_enactment_modifier = {
					name = social_banditry
					multiplier = -1
				}
			}
			1 = {
				add_enactment_modifier = {
					name = social_banditry
				}
			}
		}
		scope:bandit_state = {
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = 0.1
				strata = lower
			}
		}
	}
	option = { #
		name = tax.4.c
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = social_banditry
		}
		scope:bandit_state = {
			add_modifier = { # state_tax_waste_add
				name = poverty_relief_mod
				days = short_modifier_time
				multiplier = 2
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # triggered by emergency relief present in state, or good welfare institution
		name = tax.4.d
		highlighted_option = yes
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				institution_investment_level = {
					institution = institution_social_security
					value >= 3
				}
				scope:bandit_state = {
					has_decree = decree_emergency_relief
				}
			}
		}
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = social_banditry
		}
		scope:bandit_state = {
			add_modifier = { # state_tax_waste_add
				name = poverty_relief_mod
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
}

# Advance - Philanthropy
# Rich pops are giving to charity and supporting the law
tax.5 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT

	title = tax.5.t
	desc = tax.5.d
	flavor = tax.5.f

	category = enactment

	event_image = {
		video = "middleeast_upperclass_party"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			is_enacting_law = law_type:law_proportional_taxation
			is_enacting_law = law_type:law_graduated_taxation
			is_enacting_law = law_type:law_per_capita_based_taxation
		}
		any_scope_pop = {
			standard_of_living > 40
			strata = upper
			pop_acceptance >= acceptance_status_4
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		currently_enacting_law = {
    		save_scope_as = current_law_scope
    	}
    	random_scope_pop = {
    		limit = {
    			standard_of_living > 40
				strata = upper
				pop_acceptance >= acceptance_status_4
    		}
    		save_scope_as = philanthropists
    	}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = { currently_enacting_law = scope:current_law_scope }
	}

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	option = { # encourage philanthropy
		name = tax.5.a
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = philanthropy_law
		}
		# welfare for the poor, less expectations for the rich
		add_modifier = {
			name = philanthropy_mod
			days = short_modifier_time
		}
	}
	option = { # rally support for the law
		name = tax.5.b
		default_option = yes
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = philanthropy_law
			multiplier = 2
		}
	}
}

# Debate - Temporarily embarrassed millionaires
tax.6 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT

	title = tax.6.t
	desc = tax.6.d
	flavor = tax.6.f

	category = enactment

	event_image = {
		video = "middleeast_middleclass_cafe"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_law = law_type:law_consumption_based_taxation }
		OR = {
			is_enacting_law = law_type:law_proportional_taxation
			is_enacting_law = law_type:law_graduated_taxation
			is_enacting_law = law_type:law_per_capita_based_taxation
		}
		any_scope_pop = {
			standard_of_living < 40
			strata = middle
			pop_acceptance >= acceptance_status_4
		}
		NOT = {
			currently_enacting_law = {
				has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_short_term_tax_cuts
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		currently_enacting_law = {
    		save_scope_as = current_law_scope
    	}
    	random_scope_pop = {
    		limit = {
    			standard_of_living < 40
				strata = middle
				pop_acceptance >= acceptance_status_4
    		}
    		save_scope_as = middle_class_pop
    	}
		ig:ig_petty_bourgeoisie ?= {
			save_scope_as = pb_ig
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = { currently_enacting_law = scope:current_law_scope }
	}

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	option = { # lose some progress, middle class have higher expectations
		name = tax.6.a
		default_option = yes
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = temporary_embarrassment
			multiplier = 0.5
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = temporary_embarrassment_pops
			days = short_modifier_time
		}
		add_radicals = {
			strata = middle
			value = 0.05
		}
	}
	option = { # reduce middle class taxes, add some success chance
		name = tax.6.b
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = temporary_embarrassment
		}
		currently_enacting_law = {
			add_amendment = {
				type = amendment_short_term_tax_cuts
				sponsor = scope:pb_ig
				cooldown = 30
			}
		}
	}
}
﻿namespace = technocracy

# Prominent technocrat makes poor business decision and has a public meltdown
technocracy.1 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT

	title = technocracy.1.t
	desc = technocracy.1.d
	flavor = technocracy.1.f

	category = enactment

	event_image = {
		video = "middleeast_courtroom_upheaval"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		is_enacting_law = law_type:law_technocracy
		any_interest_group = {
			ig_counts_as_marginal = no
			OR = {
				is_interest_group_type = ig_industrialists
				is_interest_group_type = ig_intelligentsia
			}
			any_scope_pop = {
				is_pop_type = capitalists
			}
			law_stance = {
				law = law_type:law_technocracy
				value >= approve
			}
			leader = {
				is_immortal = no
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		currently_enacting_law = {
			save_scope_as = current_law_scope
		}
		random_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				ig_counts_as_marginal = no
				OR = {
					is_interest_group_type = ig_industrialists
					is_interest_group_type = ig_intelligentsia
				}
				any_scope_pop = {
					is_pop_type = capitalists
				}
				law_stance = {
					law = law_type:law_technocracy
					value >= approve
				}
				leader = {
					is_immortal = no
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = technocrat_ig
			leader = {
				save_scope_as = technocrat_leader
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_technocracy }
	}

	option = { # Bail him out
		name = technocracy.1.a
		default_option = yes
		add_enactment_modifier = { # lose 10%
			name = foolish_technocrat
			multiplier = 2
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = bailed_out_technocrat
			multiplier = money_amount_multiplier_small
			days = short_modifier_time
		}
		scope:technocrat_leader = {
			add_modifier = { # lose popularity
				name = disgraced_technocrat
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # What a disgrace
		name = technocracy.1.b
		add_enactment_modifier = { # lose 20%
			name = foolish_technocrat
			multiplier = 4
		}
		scope:technocrat_leader = {
			add_modifier = { # lose popularity
				name = disgraced_technocrat
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Have him committed to the asylum
		name = technocracy.1.c
		highlighted_option = yes
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_law = law_type:law_outlawed_dissent
				has_law = law_type:law_secret_police
				scope:technocrat_leader = {
					OR = {
						has_trait = senile
						has_trait = syphilis
						has_trait = psychological_affliction
					}
				}
			}
		}
		add_enactment_modifier = { # lose 5%
			name = foolish_technocrat
		}
		scope:technocrat_leader = {
			kill_character = yes
		}
	}
}

# advance event - The New Machine
technocracy.2 = {
	type = country_event

	title = technocracy.2.t
	desc = technocracy.2.d
	flavor = technocracy.2.f

	category = enactment
	placement = scope:new_machine_state

	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_gears_pistons"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_industry.dds"

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		is_enacting_law = law_type:law_technocracy
		any_scope_state = {
			is_incorporated = yes
			any_scope_pop = {
				is_pop_type = engineers
				OR = {
					pop_employment_building_group = bg_light_industry
					pop_employment_building_group = bg_heavy_industry
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		currently_enacting_law = {
			save_scope_as = current_law_scope
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				is_incorporated = yes
				any_scope_pop = {
					is_pop_type = engineers
					OR = {
						pop_employment_building_group = bg_light_industry
						pop_employment_building_group = bg_heavy_industry
					}
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = new_machine_state
			random_scope_pop = {
				limit = {
					is_pop_type = engineers
					OR = {
						pop_employment_building_group = bg_light_industry
						pop_employment_building_group = bg_heavy_industry
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = new_machine_pop
				workplace = {
					save_scope_as = new_machine_building
				}
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_technocracy }
	}

	option = { # Apply these ideas to local industries
		name = technocracy.2.a
		default_option = yes
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = new_machine_enactment
		}
		scope:new_machine_state = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = new_machine_state_mod
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Focus on their political ideas
		name = technocracy.2.b
		trigger = {
			enactment_phase < 2
		}
		highlighted_option = yes
		add_enactment_phase = 1
		scope:new_machine_building = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = new_machine_building_mod
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
}

# University debate
technocracy.3 = {
	type = country_event

	title = technocracy.3.t
	desc = technocracy.3.d
	flavor = technocracy.3.f

	category = enactment
	placement = scope:university_state

	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_politicians_arguing"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_protest.dds"

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		is_enacting_law = law_type:law_technocracy
		any_scope_state = {
			is_incorporated = yes
			has_building = building_university
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		currently_enacting_law = {
			save_scope_as = current_law_scope
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				is_incorporated = yes
				has_building = building_university
			}
			save_scope_as = university_state
			random_scope_building = {
				limit = {
					is_building_type = building_university
				}
				save_scope_as = university_building
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_technocracy }
	}

	option = {
		name = technocracy.3.a
		default_option = yes
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = technocracy_debate_enactment
			multiplier = 2
		}
		scope:university_state = {
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				pop_type = academics
				value = very_large_radicals
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = technocracy.3.b
		add_enactment_modifier = { # 5%
			name = technocracy_debate_enactment
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = anti_aristocratic_sentiment
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
	}
}

# Technocracy JE reward
technocracy.4 = {
	type = country_event

	title = technocracy.4.t
	desc = technocracy.4.d
	flavor = technocracy.4.f

	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_gears_pistons"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_industry.dds"

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		# Triggered by JE completion
	}

	immediate = {
		ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= {
			save_scope_as = intelligentsia_ig
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = technocracy.4.a
		default_option = yes
		add_modifier = {
			name = modifier_technocracy_brave_new_world
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
		scope:intelligentsia_ig = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_republican
				}
				remove_ideology = ideology_republican
				add_ideology = ideology_technocratic
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_constitutionalist
				}
				remove_ideology = ideology_constitutionalist
				add_ideology = ideology_technocratic
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = technocracy.4.b
		add_modifier = {
			name = modifier_technocracy_scientific_management
			days = long_modifier_time
		}
		scope:intelligentsia_ig = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_republican
				}
				remove_ideology = ideology_republican
				add_ideology = ideology_technocratic
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_constitutionalist
				}
				remove_ideology = ideology_constitutionalist
				add_ideology = ideology_technocratic
			}
		}
	}
}

# Advancement - Miracle Workers
theocracy.1 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT

	title = theocracy.1.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = { # health
				desc = theocracy.1.d1
				trigger = {
					has_law = law_type:law_charitable_health_system
					institution_investment_level = {
						institution = institution_health_system
						value >= 2
					}
				}
			}
			triggered_desc = { # schools
				desc = theocracy.1.d2
				trigger = {
					has_law = law_type:law_religious_schools
					institution_investment_level = {
						institution = institution_schools
						value >= 2
					}
				}
			}
			triggered_desc = { # neither
				desc = theocracy.1.d3
				trigger = {
					always = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
	flavor = theocracy.1.f

	category = enactment

	event_image = {
		trigger = {
			religion = rel:jewish
		}
		video = "europenorthamerica_judaism"
	}
	event_image = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				religion = rel:protestant
				religion = rel:catholic
				religion = rel:orthodox
				religion = rel:oriental_orthodox
			}
		}
		video = "southamerica_christianity"
	}
	event_image = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				religion = rel:hindu
				religion = rel:sikh
			}
		}
		video = "asia_hinduism_sikhism"
	}
	event_image = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				religion = rel:shinto
				religion = rel:confucian
			}
		}
		video = "asia_confucianism_shinto"
	}
	event_image = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				religion = rel:mahayana
				religion = rel:gelugpa
				religion = rel:theravada
			}
		}
		video = "asia_buddhism"
	}
	event_image = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				religion = rel:sunni
				religion = rel:shiite
			}
		}
		video = "middleeast_islam"
	}
	event_image = {
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				religion = rel:jewish
				religion = rel:protestant
				religion = rel:catholic
				religion = rel:orthodox
				religion = rel:oriental_orthodox
				religion = rel:mahayana
				religion = rel:gelugpa
				religion = rel:theravada
				religion = rel:shinto
				religion = rel:hindu
				religion = rel:sikh
				religion = rel:sunni
				religion = rel:shiite
			}
		}
		video = "africa_animism"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			is_enacting_law = law_type:law_theocracy
			is_enacting_law = law_type:law_state_religion
		}
		NOT = {
			has_modifier = miracle_workers_mod
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		currently_enacting_law = {
			save_scope_as = current_law_scope
		}
		ig:ig_devout ?= {
			save_scope_as = priests_scope
		}
		capital = {
			save_scope_as = capital_scope
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = { currently_enacting_law = scope:current_law_scope }
	}

	option = { # focus on the institution
		name = theocracy.1.a
		highlighted_option = yes
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					has_law = law_type:law_charitable_health_system
					institution_investment_level = {
						institution = institution_health_system
						value >= 2
					}
				}
				AND = {
					has_law = law_type:law_religious_schools
					institution_investment_level = {
						institution = institution_schools
						value >= 2
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_charitable_health_system
				institution_investment_level = {
					institution = institution_health_system
					value >= 2
				}
			}
			capital = {
				add_modifier = {
					name = miraculous_healing
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
			# success chance
			add_enactment_modifier = {
				name = miracle_workers_mod
			}
		}
		else = {
			capital = {
				add_modifier = {
					name = religious_education_modifier
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
			# success chance
			add_enactment_modifier = {
				name = miracle_workers_mod
				multiplier = 3
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # focus on the devout
		name = theocracy.1.b
		# success chance
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = miracle_workers_mod
			multiplier = 2
		}
		# devout pop attraction
		ig:ig_devout ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = miracle_workers_ig_mod
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # focus on the law
		name = theocracy.1.c
		default_option = yes
		# success chance
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = miracle_workers_mod
			multiplier = 4
		}
	}
}

# Debate Event - Blasphemy

	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = theocracy.2.t
	desc = theocracy.2.d
	flavor = theocracy.2.f

	category = enactment

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	event_image = {
		trigger = {
			religion = rel:jewish
		}
		video = "europenorthamerica_judaism"
	}
	event_image = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				religion = rel:protestant
				religion = rel:catholic
				religion = rel:orthodox
				religion = rel:oriental_orthodox
			}
		}
		video = "southamerica_christianity"
	}
	event_image = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				religion = rel:hindu
				religion = rel:sikh
			}

	}

				religion = rel:confucian

		}

	}
	event_image = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				religion = rel:mahayana
				religion = rel:gelugpa
				religion = rel:theravada
			}
		}
		video = "asia_buddhism"
	}
	event_image = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				religion = rel:sunni
				religion = rel:shiite
			}
		}
		video = "middleeast_islam"
	}
	event_image = {
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				religion = rel:jewish
				religion = rel:protestant
				religion = rel:catholic
				religion = rel:orthodox
				religion = rel:oriental_orthodox
				religion = rel:mahayana
				religion = rel:gelugpa
				religion = rel:theravada
				religion = rel:shinto
				religion = rel:hindu
				religion = rel:sikh
				religion = rel:sunni
				religion = rel:shiite
			}
		}
		video = "africa_animism"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_scales.dds"

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			is_enacting_law = law_type:law_theocracy
			is_enacting_law = law_type:law_state_religion
		}
		any_scope_state = {
			is_incorporated = yes
			any_scope_pop = {
				religion = {
					is_state_religion = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
		NOR = {
			has_modifier = blasphemy_laws
			has_modifier = relaxed_blasphemy_laws
		}
		NOT = {
			currently_enacting_law = {
				has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_draconian_blasphemy_laws
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		currently_enacting_law = {
			save_scope_as = current_law_scope
		}
		ig:ig_devout ?= {
			save_scope_as = priests_scope
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_pop = {
					religion = {
						is_state_religion = ROOT
					}
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = blasphemy_state
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = { currently_enacting_law = scope:current_law_scope }
	}

	option = {
		name = theocracy.2.a
		default_option = yes
		# enactment time reduced
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = blasphemy_laws
		}
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				scope:blasphemy_state = {
					add_radicals_in_state = {
						religion = ROOT.religion
						value = medium_radicals
					}
				}
			}
			50 = {
				scope:blasphemy_state = {
					add_loyalists_in_state = {
						religion = ROOT.religion
						value = medium_radicals
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = theocracy.2.b
		# lose success chance
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = relaxed_blasphemy_laws
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = theocracy.2.c
		highlighted_option = yes
		trigger = {
			has_law = law_type:law_outlawed_dissent
		}
		currently_enacting_law = {
			add_amendment = {
				type = amendment_draconian_blasphemy_laws
				sponsor = scope:priests_scope
				cooldown = 60
			}
		}
		# enactment speed
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = blasphemy_laws
		}
		scope:blasphemy_state = {
			add_loyalists_in_state = {
				religion = ROOT.religion
				value = 0.1
			}
		}
	}
}

# Stall event - religious pogroms
theocracy.3 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT

	title = theocracy.3.t
	desc = theocracy.3.d
	flavor = theocracy.3.f

	category = enactment

	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_vandalized_storefront"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_skull.dds"

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			is_enacting_law = law_type:law_theocracy
			is_enacting_law = law_type:law_state_religion
		}
		any_scope_state = {
			is_incorporated = yes
			any_scope_pop = {
				PREV = {
					state_religious_acceptance = {
						target = PREV.religion
						value < religious_acceptance_medium
					}
				}
			}
			any_scope_pop = {
				religion = ROOT.religion
				pop_has_primary_culture = yes
			}
		}
		NOR = {
			has_modifier = pogroms_law_success
			has_modifier = pogroms_law_time
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		currently_enacting_law = {
			save_scope_as = current_law_scope
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				is_incorporated = yes
				any_scope_pop = {
					PREV = {
						state_religious_acceptance = {
							target = PREV.religion
							value < religious_acceptance_medium
						}
					}
				}
				any_scope_pop = {
					religion = ROOT.religion
					pop_has_primary_culture = yes
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = pogrom_state
			random_scope_pop = {
				limit = {
					religion = ROOT.religion
					pop_has_primary_culture = yes
				}
				save_scope_as = perpetrator_pop
			}
			random_scope_pop = {
				limit = {
					PREV = {
						state_religious_acceptance = {
							target = PREV.religion
							value < religious_acceptance_medium
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_pop
				religion = {
					save_scope_as = target_religion
				}
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = { currently_enacting_law = scope:current_law_scope }
	}

	option = {
		name = theocracy.3.a
		default_option = yes
		# massively lose success chance
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = pogroms_law_success
		}
		scope:pogrom_state = {
			add_religion_standard_of_living_modifier = {
				religion = scope:target_religion
				multiplier = -4
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = theocracy.3.b
		# massively slow down enactment
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = pogroms_law_time
			multiplier = 2
		}
		scope:pogrom_state = {
			add_religion_standard_of_living_modifier = {
				religion = scope:target_religion
				multiplier = -2
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = theocracy.3.c
		highlighted_option = yes
		trigger = {
			institution_investment_level = {
				institution = institution_police
				value >= 3
			}
		}
		# less slow enactment
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = pogroms_law_time
		}
		scope:pogrom_state = {
			add_religion_standard_of_living_modifier = {
				religion = scope:target_religion
				multiplier = -1
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
}
﻿namespace = unable_to_enact

# Unable to continue enacting law
unable_to_enact.1 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = unable_to_enact.1.t
	desc = unable_to_enact.1.d
	flavor = unable_to_enact.1.f

	category = enactment

	duration = 3

	event_image = {
		video = "africa_leader_arguing"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_default.dds"

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_variable = legislative_failures_event }
		enacting_any_law = yes
		enactment_chance <= 0
		num_pending_events:enactment = 0
	}

	immediate = {
		set_variable = { name = legislative_failures_event days = 100 }

		currently_enacting_law = {
			save_scope_as = current_law_scope
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = { currently_enacting_law = scope:current_law_scope }
	}

	option = {
		name = unable_to_enact.1.a
		default_option = yes
		trigger = {
			enactment_setback_count < max_setbacks_minus_one
			enactment_chance_without_enactment_modifier > 0
		}
		add_enactment_setback = 1
		clear_enactment_modifier = yes

		remove_variable = legislative_failures_event
	}

	option = {
		name = unable_to_enact.1.b
		default_option = yes
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				enactment_setback_count > 1
				enactment_chance_without_enactment_modifier <= 0
			}
		}
		fallback = yes
		cancel_enactment = yes

		remove_variable = legislative_failures_event
	}
}

	trigger = {
		# Triggered by law_notification.4
	}

	immediate = {
		currently_enacting_law = {
			save_scope_as = current_law_scope
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = { currently_enacting_law = scope:current_law_scope }
	}

	option = {
		name = unable_to_enact.2.a
		default_option = yes
		cancel_enactment = yes
	}
}

# Houses of Faith
welfare_laws.1 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = welfare_laws.1.t
	desc = welfare_laws.1.d
	flavor = welfare_laws.1.f

	category = enactment

	duration = 3

	event_image = {
		trigger = {
			religion = rel:jewish
		}
		video = "europenorthamerica_judaism"
	}
	event_image = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				religion = rel:protestant
				religion = rel:catholic
				religion = rel:orthodox
				religion = rel:oriental_orthodox
			}
		}
		video = "southamerica_christianity"
	}
	event_image = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				religion = rel:hindu
				religion = rel:sikh
			}
		}
		video = "asia_hinduism_sikhism"
	}
	event_image = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				religion = rel:shinto
				religion = rel:confucian
			}
		}
		video = "asia_confucianism_shinto"
	}
	event_image = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				religion = rel:mahayana
				religion = rel:gelugpa
				religion = rel:theravada
			}
		}
		video = "asia_buddhism"
	}
	event_image = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				religion = rel:sunni
				religion = rel:shiite
			}
		}
		video = "middleeast_islam"
	}
	event_image = {
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				religion = rel:jewish
				religion = rel:protestant
				religion = rel:catholic
				religion = rel:orthodox
				religion = rel:oriental_orthodox
				religion = rel:mahayana
				religion = rel:gelugpa
				religion = rel:theravada
				religion = rel:shinto
				religion = rel:hindu
				religion = rel:sikh
				religion = rel:sunni
				religion = rel:shiite
			}
		}
		video = "africa_animism"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"


	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_default.dds"

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		is_enacting_law = law_type:law_poor_laws
		NOT = { has_modifier = promise_religious_workhouses }
		NOT = { has_modifier = promise_secular_workhouses }
		any_scope_pop = {
			state = {
				state_religious_acceptance = {
					target = PREV.religion
					value < religious_acceptance_low
				}
			}
		}
		NOT = {
			currently_enacting_law = {
				has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_religious_workhouses
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		currently_enacting_law = {
			save_scope_as = current_law_scope
		}
		ig:ig_devout ?= {
			save_scope_as = devout_ig
		}
		random_scope_pop = {
			limit = {
				state = {
					state_religious_acceptance = {
						target = PREV.religion
						value < religious_acceptance_low
					}
				}
			}
			religion = {
				save_scope_as = discriminated_religion_scope
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = { currently_enacting_law = scope:current_law_scope }
	}

	option = {
		name = welfare_laws.1.a
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = promise_religious_workhouses
		}
		currently_enacting_law = {
			add_amendment = {
				type = amendment_religious_workhouses
				sponsor = scope:devout_ig
				cooldown = 120
			}
		}
		add_radicals = {
			value = large_radicals
			religion = scope:discriminated_religion_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = welfare_laws.1.b
		default_option = yes
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = promise_secular_workhouses
		}
	}
}


# The Principle of Population
welfare_laws.2 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = welfare_laws.2.t
	desc = welfare_laws.2.d
	flavor = welfare_laws.2.f

	category = enactment

	duration = 3

	event_image = {
		video = "europenorthamerica_rich_and_poor"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_default.dds"

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		is_enacting_law = law_type:law_poor_laws
		NOT = { has_modifier = malthusian_arguments }
		NOT = { has_modifier = anti_malthusian_arguments }
		NOT = {
			currently_enacting_law = {
				has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_malthusian_foundations
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		currently_enacting_law = {
			save_scope_as = current_law_scope
		}
		ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= {
			save_scope_as = malthusian_ig
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = { currently_enacting_law = scope:current_law_scope }
	}

	option = {
		name = welfare_laws.2.a
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = malthusian_arguments
		}
		currently_enacting_law = {
			add_amendment = {
				type = amendment_malthusian_foundations
				sponsor = scope:malthusian_ig
				cooldown = 120
			}
		}
		add_radicals = {
			value = small_radicals
			strata = lower
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = welfare_laws.2.b
		default_option = yes
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = anti_malthusian_arguments
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = welfare_laws.2.c
		trigger = {
			enactment_setback_count < max_setbacks_minus_one
		}
		highlighted_option = yes
		add_enactment_setback = 1
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = anti_malthusian_arguments
			multiplier = -1
		}
		add_radicals = {
			value = medium_radicals
			strata = upper
		}
	}
}

		OR = {
			is_enacting_law = law_type:law_no_social_security
			is_enacting_law = law_type:law_wage_subsidies
			is_enacting_law = law_type:law_old_age_pension
		}
		NOT = { has_modifier = child_testinomies }
		NOT = { has_modifier = child_testimony }
	}

			name = child_testinomies

					value < neutral
				}
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = workhouse_children_outrage

		}
	}

	option = {

# The Price of War
welfare_laws.8 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = welfare_laws.8.t
	desc = welfare_laws.8.d
	flavor = welfare_laws.8.f

	category = enactment

	duration = 3

	event_image = {
		video = "middleeast_battlefield_trenches"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_default.dds"

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		is_enacting_law = law_type:law_old_age_pension
		NOT = { has_modifier = veteran_support }
		NOT = { has_modifier = general_support }
		NOT = {
			currently_enacting_law = {
				has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_veterans_pensions
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		currently_enacting_law = {
			save_scope_as = current_law_scope
		}
		ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
			save_scope_as = armed_forces_ig
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = { currently_enacting_law = scope:current_law_scope }
	}

	option = {
		name = welfare_laws.8.a
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = veteran_support
		}
		currently_enacting_law = {
			add_amendment = {
				type = amendment_veterans_pensions
				sponsor = scope:armed_forces_ig
				cooldown = 120
			}
		}
		ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = veterans_prioritized
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		ig:ig_trade_unions ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = veterans_prioritized
				days = normal_modifier_time
				multiplier = -1
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = welfare_laws.8.b
		default_option = yes
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = general_support
		}
	}
}

# Unionized Leverage
welfare_laws.9 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = welfare_laws.9.t
	desc = welfare_laws.9.d
	flavor = welfare_laws.9.f

	category = enactment

	duration = 3

	event_image = {
		video = "southamerica_aristocrats"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_default.dds"

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			is_enacting_law = law_type:law_wage_subsidies
			is_enacting_law = law_type:law_old_age_pension
		}
		ig:ig_industrialists ?= { # To account for woke capitalism
			law_enactment_stance = {
				law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type
				value < neutral
			}
		}
		NOT = { has_modifier = pro_union_argument }
		NOT = { has_modifier = anti_union_argument }
		NOT = {
			currently_enacting_law = {
				has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_welfare_pro_employer_bias
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		currently_enacting_law = {
			save_scope_as = current_law_scope
		}
		ig:ig_industrialists ?= {
			save_scope_as = industrialists_ig
		}
		ig:ig_trade_unions ?= {
			save_scope_as = trade_unions_ig
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = { currently_enacting_law = scope:current_law_scope }
	}

	option = {
		name = welfare_laws.9.a
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = pro_union_argument
		}
		ig:ig_industrialists ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = embraced_union_leverage
				days = normal_modifier_time
				multiplier = -2
			}
		}
		ig:ig_trade_unions ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = embraced_union_leverage
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = welfare_laws.9.b
		default_option = yes
		currently_enacting_law = {
			add_amendment = {
				type = amendment_welfare_pro_employer_bias
				sponsor = scope:industrialists_ig
				cooldown = 120
			}
		}
		ig:ig_industrialists ?= {
			improve_law_stance = yes
			add_modifier = {
				name = repudiated_union_leverage
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		ig:ig_trade_unions ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = repudiated_union_leverage
				days = normal_modifier_time
				multiplier = -1
			}
		}
	}
}

# Ruler Urges Caution
welfare_laws.10 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = welfare_laws.10.t
	desc = welfare_laws.10.d
	flavor = welfare_laws.10.f

	category = enactment

	duration = 3

	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_ruler_speaking_to_people"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_default.dds"

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			is_enacting_law = law_type:law_wage_subsidies
			is_enacting_law = law_type:law_old_age_pension
		}
		NOT = {	has_modifier = rejected_austerity_argument }
		NOT = {	has_modifier = accepted_austerity_argument }
		tax_level > medium
		ruler = {
			interest_group = {
				NOT = {
					law_stance = {
						law = root.currently_enacting_law.type
						value > neutral
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		currently_enacting_law = {
			save_scope_as = current_law_scope
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = { currently_enacting_law = scope:current_law_scope }
	}

	option = {
		name = welfare_laws.10.a
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = rejected_austerity_argument
		}
		ruler = {
			interest_group = {
				add_modifier = {
					name = overspending_on_welfare
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = welfare_laws.10.b
		default_option = yes
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = accepted_austerity_argument
		}
	}
}
